<script>
(function () {
    var AJAX_URL  = 'https://ndm.edu/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php';
    var NONCE     = '0d6587e959';

    /* ── Search autocomplete (event-delegated so it survives AJAX swaps) ── */
    var searchTimer, searchXhr;
    document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
        if (!e.target.matches('#ev-arch-search-input')) return;
        var input = e.target;
        var drop  = document.getElementById('ev-arch-autocomplete');
        if (!drop) return;
        clearTimeout(searchTimer);
        var q = input.value.trim();
        drop.innerHTML = '';
        drop.classList.remove('is-open');
        if (q.length < 2) return;
        searchTimer = setTimeout(function () {
            if (searchXhr) searchXhr.abort();
            searchXhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
            searchXhr.open('GET', AJAX_URL + '?action=ev_arch_search&nonce=' + NONCE + '&q=' + encodeURIComponent(q));
            searchXhr.onload = function () {
                if (searchXhr.status !== 200) return;
                try { var results = JSON.parse(searchXhr.responseText); } catch (e) { return; }
                drop.innerHTML = '';
                if (!results.length) return;
                results.forEach(function (r) {
                    var a = document.createElement('a');
                    a.className = 'ev-arch-ac-item';
                    a.href = r.url;
                    a.textContent = r.title;
                    drop.appendChild(a);
                });
                drop.classList.add('is-open');
            };
            searchXhr.send();
        }, 220);
    });

    /* ── AJAX listing swap — tabs, sidebar filters, pagination ─────────── */
    var main = document.getElementById('ev-arch-main');
    if (!main) return;

    function loadSection(url, push) {
        main.style.opacity = '0.4';
        main.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
        fetch(url)
            .then(function (r) { return r.text(); })
            .then(function (html) {
                var doc     = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
                var newMain = doc.getElementById('ev-arch-main');
                if (newMain) {
                    main.innerHTML = newMain.innerHTML;
                    if (push) history.pushState({ view: 'list' }, '', url);
                    var top = main.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.pageYOffset - 100;
                    window.scrollTo({ top: Math.max(0, top), behavior: 'smooth' });
                }
            })
            .catch(function () {})
            .finally(function () {
                main.style.opacity = '';
                main.style.pointerEvents = '';
            });
    }

    /* ── Month view: fetch page, extract TEC calendar, inject inline ──── */
    var _tribeCSS = false;

    function loadMonthView(url) {
        var listDiv = document.getElementById('ev-arch-events-list');
        var wrapDiv = document.getElementById('ev-arch-month-wrap');
        if (!wrapDiv) return;

        if (listDiv) listDiv.style.display = 'none';
        wrapDiv.style.display = 'block';
        wrapDiv.removeAttribute('aria-hidden');
        wrapDiv.innerHTML = '<p class="ev-arch-month-loading">Loading calendar…</p>';

        fetch(url)
            .then(function (r) { return r.text(); })
            .then(function (html) {
                var doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');

                /* inject TEC stylesheets once */
                if (!_tribeCSS) {
                    var existing = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('link[rel=stylesheet]'))
                                        .map(function (l) { return l.href; });
                    doc.querySelectorAll('link[rel=stylesheet]').forEach(function (link) {
                        if (!existing.includes(link.href)) {
                            var l = document.createElement('link');
                            l.rel = 'stylesheet'; l.href = link.href;
                            document.head.appendChild(l);
                            existing.push(link.href);
                        }
                    });
                    _tribeCSS = true;
                }

                /* extract TEC's calendar container — month-specific selectors first */
                var selectors = [
                    '.tribe-events--month',
                    '.tribe-events-calendar',
                    '.tribe-events-pg-template',
                    '.tribe-common.tribe-events',
                    '.tribe-common',
                    '#tribe-events',
                    '.tribe-events',
                    '[class*="tribe-events"]',
                    '[id*="tribe-events"]'
                ];
                var el = null;
                for (var si = 0; si < selectors.length; si++) {
                    el = doc.querySelector(selectors[si]);
                    if (el) { console.log('[ev-arch] matched selector:', selectors[si]); break; }
                }
                /* last resort: grab main content area */
                if (!el) {
                    el = doc.querySelector('main') ||
                         doc.querySelector('#main') ||
                         doc.querySelector('[role="main"]') ||
                         doc.querySelector('.site-main');
                    if (el) console.log('[ev-arch] fell back to main element');
                }
                if (el) {
                    console.log('[ev-arch] injecting:', el.className || el.id);
                    wrapDiv.innerHTML = el.outerHTML;
                } else {
                    console.warn('[ev-arch] no TEC element found — body classes:', doc.body && doc.body.className);
                    wrapDiv.innerHTML = '<p class="ev-arch-month-loading">Calendar not available.</p>';
                }

                history.pushState({ view: 'month' }, '', url);
                document.querySelectorAll('.ev-arch-view-btn').forEach(function (btn) {
                    btn.classList.toggle('is-active', btn.dataset.view === 'month');
                });
            })
            .catch(function () {
                wrapDiv.innerHTML = '<p class="ev-arch-month-loading">Could not load calendar.</p>';
            });
    }

    /* intercept ALL link clicks inside the injected calendar:
       TEC's prev/next nav uses /events/month/YYYY-MM/ URLs;
       intercept any same-origin /events/ link to keep navigation inline */
    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        var wrap = document.getElementById('ev-arch-month-wrap');
        if (!wrap || !wrap.contains(e.target)) return;
        var a = e.target.closest('a[href]');
        if (!a) return;
        var href = a.getAttribute('href') || '';
        if (!href || href === '#') return;
        /* let individual event links navigate normally */
        if (/\/tribe_events\/|\/events\/[^/]+\/$/.test(href) && !/\/events\/(month|week|day)/.test(href)) return;
        /* intercept calendar nav: /events/month/, ?eventDate=, tribe_event_display=month */
        if (/\/events\/(month|week|day)\/|eventDate=|tribe_event_display=month/.test(href)) {
            e.preventDefault();
            loadMonthView(href);
        }
    });

    /* intercept clicks on any filter/tab/pagination link */
    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        var a = e.target.closest('a.ev-arch-view-btn, a.ev-arch-sb-link, a.ev-arch-pg-btn');
        if (!a) return;
        var href = a.getAttribute('href') || '';
        if (!href || href === '#') return;
        e.preventDefault();
        if (a.dataset.view === 'month') {
            loadMonthView(href);
        } else if (/tribe_event_display=(week|day)/.test(href)) {
            /* week/day — navigate normally */
            window.location.href = href;
        } else {
            loadSection(href, true);
        }
    });

    window.addEventListener('popstate', function (e) {
        var state = (e.state || {});
        if (state.view === 'month') {
            loadMonthView(location.href);
        } else {
            loadSection(location.href, false);
        }
    });
}());
</script>

                        <div class="row footer-top-row">

const consentListeners = [];
/**
 * Called from GTM template to set callback to be executed when user consent is provided.
 * @param callback
 */
window.addConsentUpdateListener = (callback) => {
	consentListeners.push(callback);
};
document.addEventListener("cmplz_fire_categories", function (e) {
	var consentedCategories = e.detail.categories;
	const consent = {
		'security_storage': "granted",
		'functionality_storage': "granted",
		'personalization_storage':  cmplz_in_array( 'preferences', consentedCategories ) ? 'granted' : 'denied',
		'analytics_storage':  cmplz_in_array( 'statistics', consentedCategories ) ? 'granted' : 'denied',
		'ad_storage': cmplz_in_array( 'marketing', consentedCategories ) ? 'granted' : 'denied',
		'ad_user_data': cmplz_in_array( 'marketing', consentedCategories ) ? 'granted' : 'denied',
		'ad_personalization': cmplz_in_array( 'marketing', consentedCategories ) ? 'granted' : 'denied',
	};

	//don't use automatic prefixing, as the TM template needs to be sure it's cmplz_.
	let consented = [];
	for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(consent)) {
		if (value === 'granted') {
			consented.push(key);
		}
	}
	cmplz_set_cookie('cmplz_consent_mode', consented.join(','), false );
	consentListeners.forEach((callback) => {
		callback(consent);
	});
});
